- **postgres-migrations**: How to author Postgres migrations in packages/postgres under the two-phase (additive vs removal) system — additive changes go in migrations/ and run pre-deploy; destructive changes (DROP COLUMN/TABLE, RENAME) go in migrations-removal/ and run post-deploy so they never break the previous code revision mid-rollout. Use whenever creating, editing, moving, or reviewing a migration in packages/postgres, deciding which directory a migration belongs in, or touching the boxel_index / boxel_index_pending index tables.
